# Art review queue

## Naruto skills round 2: Four-Tails beam (2026-10-18)

The user changed the ultimate to the Four-Tails fox firing a beam from its mouth (relay skills #5). v14 remains preserved with its historical approval and sidecar, but is superseded and will not ship as `beamFire`. New drawing candidates are unapproved. The effects are proposed under `art/vfx/raw/_proposed/` until Claude lands packer support; therefore no manifest entry or packed claim is made yet. The beam charge shown below is the branch's latest seam-repaired v17; v12 remains preserved in history. Review gallery: [round 2 candidate set](../public/reviews/naruto-skills-round2/index.html).

## Goku/Luffy full core approval via Claude relay #14 (2026-10-16)

The user's exact answer was **"Approve all 16 Goku strips and all 19 Luffy strips as drawn."** Every version below is approved by the user via Claude. Earlier approvals remain in force, so the two complete core sets now have user drawing approval. The source versions and branch manifests are on unmerged PRs #123 / #96. No new image calls, source changes, merge or production deployment are implied.

Reviewed pages: [Goku full core set](https://gcd-tourney-le661omg1-eshin087s-projects.vercel.app/characters/goku/core-review-final/index.html) and [Luffy full core set](https://gcd-tourney-el0gxmsi5-eshin087s-projects.vercel.app/characters/luffy/final-core-review/index.html). Final Astra r5/r6 anatomy audits and measurement uncertainty remain applicable to the unchanged atlases. Known runtime/contact/grounding and placement warnings remain Claude's integration checks.

## Naruto skills batch 1 approval via Claude relay skills #4 (2026-10-18)
The user approved beamFire v14 with its placement sidecar and ui_ult_fg v04 via Claude relay skills #4. That approval of the pounce is historical: v14 is now superseded for the ultimate by the round-2 mouth-beam direction; ui_ult_fg v04 remains approved. The user said: “Approve beamFire v14 (with its sidecar) and ui_ult_fg v04. Don't approve beamCharge v11 or fourTailsRoar v01 yet: clean the tail seams in beamCharge f0/f2 and keep the roar burst round, as edits with no new image calls, then show me those two again.” beamCharge v17 and fourTailsRoar v02 remain unapproved and await review. No new image calls were made.

## How it works

1. **Codex generates** an image (or the user generates one in ChatGPT and hands it over) and saves it
   under the next free version name, for example `characters/naruto/reference/model_sheet_v03.png` or
   `characters/sasuke/raw/light1_v02.png`. The next number is 1 + the highest number used for that
   asset anywhere (its folder, `raw/_parts/`, `_rejected/`, and this table). Numbers are never reused.
   Codex makes up to 3 attempts per round and saves only the best one as the next version.
2. **Codex adds a row at the top of the table** with status `needs review`: date, character, asset,
   a link to the file, the version, what changed compared with the previous version, and QA notes
   (pose count, proportions, palette count, packer warnings, contact-zone check). In the same edit it
   sets the asset's `version` in `manifest.json` to that number (and `status` to `raw` if it was
   `todo`), and updates `TRACKER.md`.
3. **The user reviews.** On a phone, use the [HTTPS art review gallery](https://gcd-tourney.vercel.app/art-review.html) for playback, frame stepping and full-resolution originals. Open the linked file locally, or check a packed strip in game with `?art=custom` or in
   the Sprite Lab (`/sprites.html?c=<id>`, click "Load full sheet (.png + .json)…" and pick
   `public/characters/<id>/sheet.png` and `anims.json`; otherwise it shows the baked placeholder), and
   decide:
   - reply in chat, for example "approve naruto model_sheet v03" or "reject sasuke light1 v02: fist
     too low", or
   - edit the status cell in this file to `approved` or `rejected` and write the reason in QA notes.
4. **Codex records the decision:**
   - **approved**: set the row to `approved`; set `approvedVersion` in `manifest.json` (and `status`
     `approved`); update `TRACKER.md`. For references, copy the file to its fixed name
     (`reference/model_sheet.png`, `reference/expressions.png`); for `idle`, also copy the strip to
     `reference/idle_approved.png`; for UI, copy it to `ui/<name>.png` (shared UI: clean it into
     `art/ui/final/`). Any other `needs review` row of the same asset becomes `rejected` (reason:
     superseded by vNN) and its file moves to `_rejected/`. Older approved or rejected candidates stay
     where they are.
   - **rejected**: set the row to `rejected` with the reason; move the file into the `_rejected/`
     folder next to it (`raw/_rejected/`, `reference/_rejected/`, `art/vfx/raw/_rejected/`,
     `art/ui/raw/_rejected/`) and fix the link in the row, so the highest version left in the folder
     is always the current candidate. The next attempt is a new, higher version.
5. Keep rows forever: they are the history of the art.

Naruto and Sasuke can be produced in parallel (separate Codex threads). Each thread only adds and
edits rows for its own character. Re-read this file right before editing it and insert new rows at
the top, so two threads do not overwrite each other.

Status values: `needs review` · `approved` · `rejected`.
